```css
.page-header {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.page-title {
  font-size: 1.875rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}

.page-subtitle {
  color: var(--text-secondary);
}

.header-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 1rem;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.btn-icon {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
}

.text-red { color: var(--danger-color); border-color: var(--danger-color); }
.text-red:hover { background-color: #fee2e2; }

.text-green-btn { background-color: var(--success-color); }
.text-green-btn:hover { background-color: #059669; }

.card-header {
  margin-bottom: 1.5rem;
}

.search-box {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  background-color: var(--bg-color);
  border: 1px solid var(--border-color);
  border-radius: 0.5rem;
  padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
  width: 300px;
}

.search-icon {
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  margin-right: 0.5rem;
}

.search-input {
  border: none;
  background: transparent;
  outline: none;
  width: 100%;
  color: var(--text-primary);
}

.table-container {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.type-badge {
  padding: 0.25rem 0.5rem;
  border-radius: 1rem;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.badge-in { background-color: #d1fae5; color: #16a34a; }
.badge-out { background-color: #fee2e2; color: #dc2626; }

.text-green { color: #16a34a; font-weight: 600; }
.text-red { color: #dc2626; font-weight: 600; }

.text-center { text-align: center; }
.py-4 { padding-top: 1rem; padding-bottom: 1rem; }
.text-secondary { color: var(--text-secondary); }

@media (max-width: 700px) {
  .page-header {
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 12px;
    margin-bottom: 1rem;
  }

  .page-header > div {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .page-title {
    font-size: 1.25rem;
  }

  .header-actions,
  .page-header > div:last-child {
    display: grid !important;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 8px !important;
    width: 100%;
  }

  .header-actions .btn,
  .page-header > div:last-child .btn {
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    padding-left: 10px;
    padding-right: 10px;
  }
}
```

## File: .\inventory-frontend\src\app\features\inventory\inventory.component.html
```html
<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px;">
  
  <div class="page-header">
    <div>
      <h1 class="page-title">{{ 'SIDEBAR.INVENTORY' | translate }}</h1>
      <p class="page-subtitle">{{ 'INVENTORY.SUBTITLE' | translate }}</p>
    </div>
    <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center;">
      <button class="btn btn-secondary" (click)="openActionModal('return')">
        {{ 'INVENTORY.RETURN_STOCK' | translate }}
      </button>
      <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-secondary" (click)="openActionModal('adjust')">
        {{ 'INVENTORY.ADJUST_STOCK' | translate }}
      </button>
      <button class="btn btn-danger" style="background: var(--danger-light); color: var(--danger); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.2);" (click)="openActionModal('sell')">
        {{ 'INVENTORY.SELL_STOCK' | translate }}
      </button>
      <button class="btn btn-primary" (click)="openActionModal('add')">
        <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Plus" [size]="16"></lucide-icon>
        {{ 'INVENTORY.ADD_STOCK' | translate }}
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card" style="padding: 12px 16px;">
    <div class="search-filter-row">
      
      <div class="search-wrapper" style="min-width: 220px; flex: 1; max-width: 320px;">
        <span class="search-icon"><lucide-icon [img]="icons.Search" [size]="16"></lucide-icon></span>
        <input 
          type="text" 
          [(ngModel)]="searchQuery" 
          (ngModelChange)="onFilterChange()"
          [placeholder]="'COMMON.SEARCH' | translate" 
          class="search-input"
        >
      </div>

      <div class="search-filter-controls">
        
        <select class="form-select filter-select" style="width: 180px;" [(ngModel)]="actionFilter" (ngModelChange)="onFilterChange()">
          <option value="">{{ 'INVENTORY.ACTION_FILTER' | translate }} ({{ 'COMMON.ALL' | translate }})</option>
          <option value="Add">{{ 'INVENTORY.ADD' | translate }}</option>
          <option value="Sell">{{ 'INVENTORY.SELL' | translate }}</option>
          <option value="Adjust">{{ 'INVENTORY.ADJUST' | translate }}</option>
          <option value="Return">{{ 'INVENTORY.RETURN' | translate }}</option>
        </select>

        <button class="btn btn-excel btn-sm" (click)="exportToExcel()" [disabled]="isLoading || getFilteredLogs().length === 0">
          Excel
        </button>
        <button class="btn btn-pdf btn-sm" (click)="exportToPdf()" [disabled]="isLoading || getFilteredLogs().length === 0">
          PDF
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card" style="padding: 0;">
    <div class="table-wrapper">
      <table id="inventory-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>{{ 'COMMON.ID' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.DATE' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.PRODUCT' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.ACTION' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.PREVIOUS' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.CHANGE' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.NEW' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.CHANGED_BY' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'INVENTORY.NOTES' | translate }}</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          <ng-container *ngIf="isLoading">
            <tr *ngFor="let dummy of [1,2,3,4,5]">
              <td *ngFor="let col of [1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9]">
                <div class="skeleton" style="height: 16px; width: 75%;"></div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          </ng-container>

          <tr *ngIf="!isLoading && getFilteredLogs().length === 0">
            <td colspan="9">
              <div class="empty-state">
                <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ClipboardList" [size]="40"></lucide-icon>
                <p>{{ 'INVENTORY.NO_MOVEMENTS' | translate }}</p>
              </div>
            </td>
          </tr>

          <ng-container *ngIf="!isLoading && getFilteredLogs().length > 0">
            <tr *ngFor="let log of getFilteredLogs()">
              <td [attr.data-label]="'COMMON.ID' | translate" style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-muted);">#{{ log.id }}</td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.DATE' | translate" style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted); white-space: nowrap;">
                {{ log.actionDate | date:'medium' }}
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.PRODUCT' | translate" class="font-bold">{{ log.productName }}</td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.ACTION' | translate">
                <span class="badge" 
                      [ngClass]="{
                        'badge-success': log.action === 'Add',
                        'badge-danger': log.action === 'Sell',
                        'badge-warning': log.action === 'Adjust',
                        'badge-info': log.action === 'Return'
                      }">
                  {{ ('INVENTORY.' + log.action.toUpperCase()) | translate }}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.PREVIOUS' | translate">{{ log.previousQuantity }}</td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.CHANGE' | translate" class="font-bold" 
                  [ngStyle]="{
                    'color': log.action === 'Sell' ? 'var(--danger)' : 
                             (log.action === 'Add' ? 'var(--success)' : 
                             (log.action === 'Return' ? 'var(--info)' : 'var(--warning)'))
                  }">
                {{ log.action === 'Sell' ? '-' : '+' }}{{ log.quantityChanged }}
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.NEW' | translate" class="font-bold">{{ log.newQuantity }}</td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.CHANGED_BY' | translate">
                <span class="badge badge-accent">{{ log.performedBy || '—' }}</span>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'INVENTORY.NOTES' | translate" style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-secondary); max-width: 160px; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;" [title]="log.notes">
                {{ log.notes || '—' }}
              </td>
            </tr>
          </ng-container>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    <div class="pagination" *ngIf="totalPages > 0" style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; width: 100%; padding: 16px 24px;">
      <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;">
        <span class="pagination-info" style="margin: 0;">{{ 'PRODUCTS.PAGE' | translate }} {{ page }} / {{ totalPages }}</span>
        <select class="form-select" style="width: auto; padding: 4px 30px 4px 10px; font-size: 13px;" [(ngModel)]="pageSize" (ngModelChange)="onPageSizeChange()">
          <option [value]="5">5</option>
          <option [value]="10">10</option>
          <option [value]="15">15</option>
          <option [value]="25">25</option>
          <option [value]="50">50</option>
          <option [value]="100">100</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="pagination-controls">
        <button class="page-btn" [disabled]="page === 1" (click)="prevPage()">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ChevronLeft" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
        <button *ngFor="let pg of getPagesArray()" class="page-btn" [ngClass]="{ active: page === pg }" (click)="goToPage(pg)">
          {{ pg }}
        </button>
        <button class="page-btn" [disabled]="page === totalPages" (click)="nextPage()">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ChevronRight" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="custom-modal-overlay" *ngIf="actionModal" (click)="closeActionModal()">
    <div class="custom-modal" style="max-width: 440px;" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="custom-modal-header">
        <h3 class="custom-modal-title">{{ getModalTitle() | translate }}</h3>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-body" style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 14px; padding-top: 16px;">
        
        <div class="badge badge-danger" *ngIf="actionError" style="padding: 10px; border-radius: 8px; font-size: 13px;">
          {{ actionError }}
        </div>

        <div class="form-group">
          <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 6px;">
            <label class="form-label" style="margin-bottom: 0;">{{ 'INVENTORY.PRODUCT' | translate }} *</label>
            <button class="btn btn-secondary btn-sm" style="padding: 2px 8px; font-size: 11px; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px; cursor: pointer;" (click)="toggleScanner()">
              📷 {{ scanning ? 'Stop Scan' : 'Scan Barcode' }}
            </button>
          </div>

          <!-- Barcode Camera Scanner Stream -->
          <div *ngIf="scanning" style="border: 2px dashed var(--primary); border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden; margin-bottom: 10px; background: #000; position: relative;">
            <div id="barcode-reader-elem" style="width: 100%; min-height: 250px;"></div>
            <div *ngIf="scanFeedback" class="badge badge-success" style="position: absolute; bottom: 10px; left: 10px; right: 10px; z-index: 10; text-align: center;">
              {{ scanFeedback }}
            </div>
          </div>

          <select class="form-select" [(ngModel)]="selectedProductId">
            <option value="">{{ 'INVENTORY.SELECT_PRODUCT' | translate }}</option>
            <option *ngFor="let p of products" [value]="p.id">
              {{ p.name }} ({{ 'PRODUCTS.QUANTITY' | translate }}: {{ p.quantity }} {{ p.unitName }})
            </option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label">{{ 'INVENTORY.WAREHOUSE' | translate }} (Optional)</label>
          <select class="form-select" [(ngModel)]="selectedWarehouseId">
            <option value="">{{ 'INVENTORY.DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE' | translate }}</option>
            <option *ngFor="let w of warehouses" [value]="w.id">
              {{ w.name }}
            </option>
          </select>
        </div>
        
        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label">{{ getQtyLabel() | translate }} *</label>
          <input class="form-input" type="number" [min]="actionModal === 'adjust' ? 0 : 1" [(ngModel)]="quantity" />
        </div>
        
        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label">{{ 'INVENTORY.NOTES' | translate }}</label>
          <textarea class="form-textarea" rows="2" [(ngModel)]="notes"></textarea>
        </div>

      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-footer">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeActionModal()">
          {{ 'COMMON.CANCEL' | translate }}
        </button>
        <button
          class="btn btn-primary"
          [disabled]="!selectedProductId || quantity < 0 || isSubmitting"
          (click)="submitAction()"
        >
          <span class="spinner" *ngIf="isSubmitting"></span>
          {{ 'COMMON.SAVE' | translate }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

## File: .\inventory-frontend\src\app\features\inventory\inventory.component.ts
```typescript
import { Component, OnInit, ChangeDetectionStrategy, ChangeDetectorRef } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { LucideAngularModule, Search, Download, Plus, ClipboardList, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-angular';
import { InventoryService } from '../../core/services/inventory.service';
import { ProductService } from '../../core/services/product.service';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';
import { TranslatePipe } from '@ngx-translate/core';
import { ExportExcelService } from '../../core/services/export-excel.service';
import { ExportPdfService } from '../../core/services/export-pdf.service';
import { HttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { environment } from '../../../environments/environment';
import { SweetAlertService } from '../../core/services/sweetalert.service';
import { Html5Qrcode } from 'html5-qrcode';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-inventory',
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, LucideAngularModule, TranslatePipe],
  templateUrl: './inventory.component.html',
  styleUrl: './inventory.component.css',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush
})
export class InventoryComponent implements OnInit {
  readonly icons = { Search, Download, Plus, ClipboardList, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight };

  logs: any[] = [];
  products: any[] = [];
  totalCount = 0;
  totalPages = 1;
  page = 1;
  pageSize = 15;
  actionFilter = '';
  searchQuery = '';
  isLoading = false;

  user: any;

  actionModal: 'add' | 'sell' | 'adjust' | 'return' | null = null;
  isSubmitting = false;
  actionError = '';

  selectedProductId: number | '' = '';
  selectedWarehouseId: number | '' = '';
  quantity: number = 1;
  notes: string = '';

  warehouses: any[] = [];

  constructor(
    private inventoryService: InventoryService,
    private productService: ProductService,
    private authService: AuthService,
    private exportExcel: ExportExcelService,
    private exportPdf: ExportPdfService,
    private http: HttpClient,
    private sweetAlert: SweetAlertService,
    private cdr: ChangeDetectorRef
  ) {
    this.user = this.authService.getCurrentUser();
  }

  ngOnInit() {
    this.loadLogs();
    this.loadProducts();
    this.loadWarehouses();
  }

  get isAdmin() {
    const role = this.user?.['http://schemas.microsoft.com/ws/2008/06/identity/claims/role'];
    return role === 'SuperAdmin' || role === 'InventoryManager';
  }

  loadWarehouses() {
    this.http.get<any>(`${environment.apiUrl}/lookup/warehouses`).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        this.warehouses = res.data || [];
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      }
    });
  }

  loadLogs() {
    this.isLoading = true;
    this.inventoryService.getInventoryLogs(this.page, this.pageSize, this.actionFilter, this.searchQuery).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        this.logs = res.data?.items || [];
        this.totalCount = res.data?.totalCount || 0;
        this.totalPages = res.data?.totalPages || 1;
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      },
      error: () => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      }
    });
  }

  getFilteredLogs() {
    return this.logs;
  }

  onFilterChange() {
    this.page = 1;
    this.loadLogs();
  }

  onPageSizeChange() {
    this.page = 1;
    this.loadLogs();
  }

  loadProducts() {
    
    this.productService.getProducts(1, 1000).subscribe(res => {
      this.products = res.data?.items || [];
      this.cdr.markForCheck();
    });
  }

  filterByAction(action: string) {
    this.actionFilter = action;
    this.page = 1;
    this.loadLogs();
  }

  prevPage() {
    if (this.page > 1) {
      this.page--;
      this.loadLogs();
    }
  }

  nextPage() {
    if (this.page < this.totalPages) {
      this.page++;
      this.loadLogs();
    }
  }

  goToPage(pg: number) {
    this.page = pg;
    this.loadLogs();
  }

  getPagesArray() {
    const pages = [];
    const maxPages = Math.min(this.totalPages, 5);
    for (let i = 1; i <= maxPages; i++) {
      pages.push(i);
    }
    return pages;
  }

  openActionModal(action: 'add' | 'sell' | 'adjust' | 'return') {
    this.actionModal = action;
    this.selectedProductId = '';
    this.selectedWarehouseId = '';
    this.quantity = action === 'adjust' ? 0 : 1;
    this.notes = '';
    this.actionError = '';
  }

  scanning = false;
  scanFeedback = '';
  qrReader: any = null;

  toggleScanner() {
    if (this.scanning) {
      this.stopScanner();
    } else {
      this.startScanner();
    }
  }

  startScanner() {
    this.scanning = true;
    this.scanFeedback = '';
    this.cdr.markForCheck();

    setTimeout(() => {
      this.qrReader = new Html5Qrcode('barcode-reader-elem');
      this.qrReader.start(
        { facingMode: 'environment' },
        {
          fps: 10,
          qrbox: { width: 250, height: 150 }
        },
        (decodedText: string) => {
          this.scanFeedback = `Scanned: ${decodedText}`;
          this.cdr.markForCheck();

          const matched = this.products.find(p => p.barcode === decodedText || p.sku === decodedText);
          if (matched) {
            this.selectedProductId = matched.id;
            this.scanFeedback = `Found: ${matched.name}`;
            this.cdr.markForCheck();
            
            setTimeout(() => {
              this.stopScanner();
            }, 1500);
          } else {
            this.scanFeedback = `Code "${decodedText}" not matched.`;
            this.cdr.markForCheck();
          }
        },
        (errorMessage: string) => {
          // Silent
        }
      ).catch((err: any) => {
        console.error('Error starting scanner:', err);
        this.scanFeedback = 'Camera access denied or device not found.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      });
    }, 100);
  }

  stopScanner() {
    if (this.qrReader) {
      this.qrReader.stop().then(() => {
        this.scanning = false;
        this.scanFeedback = '';
        this.qrReader = null;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      }).catch((err: any) => {
        console.error('Error stopping scanner:', err);
        this.scanning = false;
        this.scanFeedback = '';
        this.qrReader = null;
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      });
    } else {
      this.scanning = false;
      this.scanFeedback = '';
      this.cdr.markForCheck();
    }
  }

  closeActionModal() {
    this.stopScanner();
    this.actionModal = null;
  }

  getModalTitle() {
    switch (this.actionModal) {
      case 'add': return 'INVENTORY.ADD_STOCK';
      case 'sell': return 'INVENTORY.SELL_STOCK';
      case 'adjust': return 'INVENTORY.ADJUST_STOCK';
      case 'return': return 'INVENTORY.RETURN_STOCK';
      default: return '';
    }
  }

  getQtyLabel() {
    switch (this.actionModal) {
      case 'add': return 'INVENTORY.QTY_TO_ADD';
      case 'sell': return 'INVENTORY.QTY_TO_SELL';
      case 'adjust': return 'INVENTORY.NEW_QTY';
      case 'return': return 'INVENTORY.QTY_TO_RETURN';
      default: return 'INVENTORY.QUANTITY_LABEL';
    }
  }

  submitAction() {
    if (!this.selectedProductId) return;
    
    this.isSubmitting = true;
    this.actionError = '';

    const payload = {
      productId: Number(this.selectedProductId),
      warehouseId: this.selectedWarehouseId ? Number(this.selectedWarehouseId) : null,
      quantity: this.quantity,
      notes: this.notes
    };

    let actionObs;
    switch (this.actionModal) {
      case 'add': 
        actionObs = this.inventoryService.addStock({
          productId: payload.productId, warehouseId: payload.warehouseId, quantityToAdd: payload.quantity, notes: payload.notes
        }); 
        break;
      case 'sell': 
        actionObs = this.inventoryService.sellStock({
          productId: payload.productId, warehouseId: payload.warehouseId, quantityToSell: payload.quantity, notes: payload.notes
        }); 
        break;
      case 'adjust': 
        actionObs = this.inventoryService.adjustStock({
          productId: payload.productId, warehouseId: payload.warehouseId, newQuantity: payload.quantity, notes: payload.notes
        }); 
        break;
      case 'return': 
        actionObs = this.inventoryService.returnStock({
          productId: payload.productId, warehouseId: payload.warehouseId, quantityToReturn: payload.quantity, notes: payload.notes
        }); 
        break;
    }

    if (!actionObs) return;

    actionObs.subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.isSubmitting = false;
        this.closeActionModal();
        this.sweetAlert.success('Success', 'Inventory action completed successfully');
        this.loadLogs();
        this.loadProducts(); 
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isSubmitting = false;
        this.actionError = err.error?.message || 'Operation failed. Please try again.';
        this.cdr.markForCheck();
      }
    });
  }

  exportToExcel() {
    const dataToExport = this.getFilteredLogs().map(log => ({
      'ID': log.id,
      'Date': new Date(log.actionDate).toLocaleString(),
      'Product Name': log.productName,
      'Action': log.action,
      'Previous Qty': log.previousQuantity,
      'Qty Change': log.quantityChanged,
      'New Qty': log.newQuantity,
      'Performed By': log.performedBy || log.userName || '—',
      'Notes': log.notes || '—'
    }));
    this.exportExcel.export(dataToExport, 'Inventory_Logs_Report');
  }

  exportToPdf() {
    this.exportPdf.export('inventory-table', 'Inventory_Logs_Report');
  }
}

```

```css
.login-wrapper {

  --border-color: rgba(28, 45, 39, 0.1);
}

}

  background: white;

  align-items: center;

}

.logo-circle img {

}

}

.hover-white:hover {

}

  border: 1px dashed rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);
  background: transparent;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.btn-outline-dashed:hover {
  border-color: white;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
}

.hero-section {
  min-height: 100vh;
  padding-top: 140px;

  background: #0f1c18; 
}

.sunset-overlay {

  top: 0;
  left: 0;

  background: linear-gradient(to bottom, #0f1c18 0%, #173832 40%, #51716d 70%, #d89871 100%);
  z-index: 0;
}

.mountains-bg {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: -2px;
  height: 45%;
  background-size: cover;
  background-position: center bottom;
  background-repeat: no-repeat;
  z-index: 1;
  opacity: 0.95;
}

.hero-title {
  font-family: 'Sora', sans-serif;
  font-weight: 800;

  line-height: 1.1;
  text-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
}

.hero-subtitle {
  max-width: 650px;
  text-shadow: 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.15);
}

.tag-badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  border: 1px dashed rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3);
  padding: 6px 16px;
  border-radius: 50px;

  letter-spacing: 0.1em;

  color: #f7d070;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.login-console {

  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(25px);
  border: 1px dashed rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15);
  border-radius: 30px;
  z-index: 2;
  position: relative;
  box-shadow: 0 30px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
}

.console-title {
  font-family: 'Sora', sans-serif;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: white;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.custom-floating .form-control {
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9) !important;
  border: 1px solid rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1) !important;
  color: #000 !important;
  border-radius: 16px;
  height: 62px;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.custom-floating .form-control:focus {

  background: #ffffff !important;
}

.custom-floating label {

}

.show-pass-btn {
  position: absolute;

}

.show-pass-btn:hover {
  color: white;
}

.btn-solid-white {
  background: white;
  color: var(--dark-green) !important;

  font-weight: 700;
}

.btn-solid-white:hover {
  background: #f7d070;
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 10px 20px rgba(247, 208, 112, 0.2);
}

.scroll-mouse {
  width: 24px;
  height: 36px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);
  border-radius: 12px;
  position: relative;
}

.scroll-wheel {
  width: 4px;
  height: 6px;
  background: white;
  border-radius: 2px;
  position: absolute;
  top: 6px;
  left: 50%;
  transform: translateX(-50%);
  animation: scrollAnim 1.5s infinite;
}

@keyframes scrollAnim {
  0% { transform: translate(-50%, 0); opacity: 1; }
  100% { transform: translate(-50%, 12px); opacity: 0; }
}

.bg-cream {
  background-color: var(--cream);
}

.text-dark-green {
  color: var(--dark-green) !important;
}

.text-dark-green-muted {
  color: var(--dark-green-muted) !important;
}

.text-accent {
  color: var(--accent) !important;
}

.badge-accent {
  background: var(--accent-soft);
  color: var(--accent) !important;
  border-radius: 50px;
  padding: 5px 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.module-card {
  border-radius: 24px !important;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px dashed var(--border-color) !important;
  background: #ffffff !important;
  transition: transform 0.3s ease, box-shadow 0.3s ease;
}

.module-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-5px);
  box-shadow: 0 20px 40px rgba(28, 45, 39, 0.06) !important;
}

.card-gradient {
  height: 140px;
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.card-wave-bg {
  position: absolute;
  bottom: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  height: 30px;
  background: #ffffff;
  
  clip-path: ellipse(60% 80% at 50% 100%);
}

.bg-grad-orange { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #f97316 0%, #ea580c 100%); }
.bg-grad-purple { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #a855f7 0%, #7e22ce 100%); }
.bg-grad-yellow { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #eab308 0%, #ca8a04 100%); }
.bg-grad-teal { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #14b8a6 0%, #0d9488 100%); }

.banner-section {
  background-color: var(--dark-green);
}

.border-white-10 {
  border-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1) !important;
}

.stat-box {
  padding: 10px;
}

.stat-box .num {
  font-family: 'Sora', sans-serif;
  font-size: 2.5rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  display: block;
  line-height: 1;
  margin-bottom: 5px;
}

.stat-box .label {
  font-size: 0.85rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
}

.steps-timeline {
  position: relative;
}

.timeline-line {
  position: absolute;
  top: 30px;
  left: 10%;
  right: 10%;
  height: 1px;
  border-top: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
  z-index: 0;
  display: none;
}

@media (min-width: 768px) {
  .timeline-line {
    display: block;
  }
}

.node-circle {
  width: 60px;
  height: 60px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: white;
  border: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
  color: var(--accent);
  font-family: 'Sora', sans-serif;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-size: 1.25rem;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  transition: all 0.3s ease;
}

.step-node:hover .node-circle {
  background: var(--accent);
  color: white;
  border-style: solid;
  transform: scale(1.1);
}

.cta-section {
  background: linear-gradient(to bottom, #d2593b 0%, #c14e30 100%);
}

.footer-section {
  background-color: #0f1c18;
}

.footer-section a {
  transition: color 0.2s ease;
}

.footer-section a:hover {
  color: white !important;
}
```

```html
<div class="login-wrapper">

  <header class="navbar navbar-expand-lg navbar-dark position-absolute top-0 start-0 w-100 z-index-3 py-3">
    <div class="container px-4">
      <a class="navbar-brand d-flex align-items-center gap-2" href="#">
        <div class="logo-circle shadow">
          <img src="assets/logo.jpg" alt="StockMaster Logo">
        </div>
        <span class="brand-text">StockMaster</span>
      </a>

      <div class="d-none d-lg-flex navbar-nav mx-auto gap-4">
        <a class="nav-link text-white-50 hover-white" (click)="scrollToOverview()">{{ 'LOGIN.PLATFORM' | translate }}</a>
        <a class="nav-link text-white-50 hover-white" (click)="scrollToOverview()">{{ 'LOGIN.FEATURES' | translate }}</a>
        <a class="nav-link text-white-50 hover-white" (click)="scrollToOverview()">{{ 'LOGIN.MODULES' | translate }}</a>
        <a class="nav-link text-white-50 hover-white" (click)="scrollToOverview()">{{ 'LOGIN.SECURITY' | translate }}</a>
      </div>

      <div class="d-flex align-items-center">
        <a href="#" class="btn btn-outline-dashed text-white rounded-pill px-4 py-2 small fw-semibold">{{ 'LOGIN.CONTACT_SUPPORT' | translate }}</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>

  <section class="hero-section position-relative d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center overflow-hidden">
    <div class="sunset-overlay"></div>
    <div class="mountains-bg" style="background-image: url('assets/sunset_mountains_bg.png');"></div>

    <div class="container hero-container position-relative z-index-2 px-4 text-center">
      <div class="row justify-content-center">
        <div class="col-xl-8 col-lg-10">

          <div class="tag-badge mb-3 mx-auto">
            <span>{{ 'LOGIN.TAGLINE' | translate }}</span>
          </div>

          <h1 class="display-3 text-white fw-bold hero-title mb-3">
            {{ 'LOGIN.HERO_TITLE' | translate }}
          </h1>

          <p class="lead text-white-50 hero-subtitle mb-5 max-w-700 mx-auto">
            {{ 'LOGIN.HERO_SUBTITLE' | translate }}
          </p>

          <div class="login-console mx-auto p-4 p-md-5 shadow-lg">
            <h3 class="console-title mb-4">{{ 'LOGIN.ACCESS_ACCOUNT' | translate }}</h3>

            <form (ngSubmit)="onSubmit()">
              <div *ngIf="error" class="alert alert-danger custom-alert d-flex align-items-center mb-4 text-start"
                role="alert">
                <lucide-icon [img]="icons.AlertCircle" [size]="18" class="me-2"></lucide-icon>
                <div>{{ error }}</div>
              </div>

              <div class="row g-3">
                <div class="col-md-6">
                  <div class="form-floating custom-floating">
                    <input type="text" class="form-control" id="usernameInput" [placeholder]="'LOGIN.USERNAME_LABEL' | translate" name="username"
                      [(ngModel)]="credentials.username" required>
                    <label for="usernameInput" class="text-white-50">
                      <lucide-icon [img]="icons.User" [size]="14" class="me-1"></lucide-icon> {{ 'LOGIN.USERNAME_LABEL' | translate }}
                    </label>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6">
                  <div class="form-floating custom-floating position-relative">
                    <input [type]="showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'" class="form-control pe-5" id="passwordInput"
                      [placeholder]="'LOGIN.PASSWORD_LABEL' | translate" name="password" [(ngModel)]="credentials.password" required>
                    <label for="passwordInput" class="text-white-50">
                      <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Lock" [size]="14" class="me-1"></lucide-icon> {{ 'LOGIN.PASSWORD_LABEL' | translate }}
                    </label>
                    <button type="button" class="btn btn-link show-pass-btn" (click)="togglePassword()">
                      <lucide-icon [img]="showPassword ? icons.EyeOff : icons.Eye" [size]="16"></lucide-icon>
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <div class="text-end mt-2">
                    <a href="javascript:void(0)" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none small hover-white" (click)="openForgotPassword()">Forgot Password?</a>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-12 mt-4">
                  <button type="submit"
                    class="btn btn-solid-white w-100 py-3 rounded-pill fw-bold text-uppercase tracking-wider"
                    [disabled]="isLoading">
                    <span *ngIf="isLoading" class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2" role="status"
                      aria-hidden="true"></span>
                    {{ isLoading ? ('LOGIN.ENTERING_PLATFORM' | translate) : ('LOGIN.LOGIN_BTN' | translate) }}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="scroll-indicator-wrap position-absolute bottom-0 start-50 translate-middle-x mb-4 z-index-2 text-center"
      (click)="scrollToOverview()">
      <span class="text-white-50 small text-uppercase tracking-wide mb-2 d-block cursor-pointer">{{ 'LOGIN.EXPLORE_PLATFORM' | translate }}</span>
      <div class="scroll-mouse mx-auto cursor-pointer">
        <div class="scroll-wheel"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section id="overview" class="overview-section py-5 bg-cream">
    <div class="container py-5 px-4">

      <div class="row align-items-end mb-5">
        <div class="col-lg-6">
          <span class="text-accent text-uppercase tracking-wide fw-bold d-block mb-2">{{ 'LOGIN.WHERE_TO_NEXT' | translate }}</span>
          <h2 class="display-5 text-dark-green fw-bold title-sora">{{ 'LOGIN.FOUR_MODULES' | translate }}</h2>
        </div>
        <div class="col-lg-6 mt-3 mt-lg-0">
          <p class="lead text-dark-green-muted mb-0">
            {{ 'LOGIN.FOUR_MODULES_DESC' | translate }}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="row g-4 mt-2">

        <div class="col-xl-3 col-md-6">
          <div class="card module-card border-0 shadow-sm h-100">
            <div class="card-gradient bg-grad-orange">
              <div class="card-wave-bg"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-4 d-flex flex-column">
              <span class="badge badge-accent mb-2">{{ 'LOGIN.CORE_MODULE' | translate }}</span>
              <h4 class="card-title fw-bold text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.STOCK_CONTROL' | translate }}</h4>
              <p class="card-text text-dark-green-muted small mb-4">
                {{ 'LOGIN.STOCK_CONTROL_DESC' | translate }}
              </p>
              <div class="d-flex flex-column mt-auto border-top pt-3">
                <span class="text-accent small fw-bold text-uppercase mb-1">{{ 'LOGIN.LIVE_TRACKING' | translate }}</span>
                <span class="text-dark-green fw-bold fs-5">99.9% Acc.</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="col-xl-3 col-md-6">
          <div class="card module-card border-0 shadow-sm h-100">
            <div class="card-gradient bg-grad-purple">
              <div class="card-wave-bg"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-4 d-flex flex-column">
              <span class="badge badge-accent mb-2">{{ 'LOGIN.INTEGRATION' | translate }}</span>
              <h4 class="card-title fw-bold text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.SUPPLIER_HUB' | translate }}</h4>
              <p class="card-text text-dark-green-muted small mb-4">
                {{ 'LOGIN.SUPPLIER_HUB_DESC' | translate }}
              </p>
              <div class="d-flex flex-column mt-auto border-top pt-3">
                <span class="text-accent small fw-bold text-uppercase mb-1">{{ 'LOGIN.SUPPLIER_SYNC' | translate }}</span>
                <span class="text-dark-green fw-bold fs-5">0 Friction</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="col-xl-3 col-md-6">
          <div class="card module-card border-0 shadow-sm h-100">
            <div class="card-gradient bg-grad-yellow">
              <div class="card-wave-bg"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-4 d-flex flex-column">
              <span class="badge badge-accent mb-2">{{ 'LOGIN.SALES' | translate }}</span>
              <h4 class="card-title fw-bold text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.SALES_LEDGER' | translate }}</h4>
              <p class="card-text text-dark-green-muted small mb-4">
                {{ 'LOGIN.SALES_LEDGER_DESC' | translate }}
              </p>
              <div class="d-flex flex-column mt-auto border-top pt-3">
                <span class="text-accent small fw-bold text-uppercase mb-1">{{ 'LOGIN.INVOICING' | translate }}</span>
                <span class="text-dark-green fw-bold fs-5">Real-time</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="col-xl-3 col-md-6">
          <div class="card module-card border-0 shadow-sm h-100">
            <div class="card-gradient bg-grad-teal">
              <div class="card-wave-bg"></div>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-4 d-flex flex-column">
              <span class="badge badge-accent mb-2">{{ 'LOGIN.ANALYTICS' | translate }}</span>
              <h4 class="card-title fw-bold text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.PROFIT_TRACKING' | translate }}</h4>
              <p class="card-text text-dark-green-muted small mb-4">
                {{ 'LOGIN.PROFIT_TRACKING_DESC' | translate }}
              </p>
              <div class="d-flex flex-column mt-auto border-top pt-3">
                <span class="text-accent small fw-bold text-uppercase mb-1">{{ 'LOGIN.REPORTS' | translate }}</span>
                <span class="text-dark-green fw-bold fs-5">1-Click PDF</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="banner-section py-5 text-center text-white position-relative">
    <div class="container py-5 px-4 z-index-2 position-relative">
      <span class="text-uppercase tracking-wider small text-white-50 mb-3 d-block">{{ 'LOGIN.THE_STOCKMASTER_WAY' | translate }}</span>
      <h2 class="display-4 fw-bold title-sora mb-5 text-white max-w-700 mx-auto">{{ 'LOGIN.BUILT_BY_DEVS' | translate }}</h2>

      <div class="row g-4 justify-content-center text-start max-w-900 mx-auto mt-4 border-top border-white-10 pt-5">
        <div class="col-md-3 col-6">
          <div class="stat-box">
            <span class="num text-white">99.9%</span>
            <span class="label text-white-50">Uptime SLA</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3 col-6">
          <div class="stat-box">
            <span class="num text-white">2.5x</span>
            <span class="label text-white-50">Logistics Velocity</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3 col-6">
          <div class="stat-box">
            <span class="num text-white">100%</span>
            <span class="label text-white-50">Secure Logs</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-md-3 col-6">
          <div class="stat-box">
            <span class="num text-white">1-Click</span>
            <span class="label text-white-50">Supplier Sync</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="steps-section py-5 bg-cream">
    <div class="container py-5 px-4 text-center">
      <span class="text-accent small text-uppercase tracking-wide fw-bold d-block mb-3">{{ 'LOGIN.HOW_WE_OPERATE' | translate }}</span>
      <h2 class="display-5 text-dark-green fw-bold title-sora mb-5">{{ 'LOGIN.HOW_CYCLE_GOES' | translate }}</h2>

      <div class="steps-timeline position-relative mt-5">
        <div class="timeline-line"></div>
        <div class="row g-4 justify-content-center">

          <div class="col-md-3">
            <div class="step-node">
              <div class="node-circle mx-auto">1</div>
              <h5 class="fw-bold mt-4 text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.RECEIVE_CARGO' | translate }}</h5>
              <p class="text-dark-green-muted small px-3">{{ 'LOGIN.RECEIVE_CARGO_DESC' | translate }}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="col-md-3">
            <div class="step-node">
              <div class="node-circle mx-auto">2</div>
              <h5 class="fw-bold mt-4 text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.SUPPLIER_SYNC_STEP' | translate }}</h5>
              <p class="text-dark-green-muted small px-3">{{ 'LOGIN.SUPPLIER_SYNC_DESC' | translate }}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="col-md-3">
            <div class="step-node">
              <div class="node-circle mx-auto">3</div>
              <h5 class="fw-bold mt-4 text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.POS_DISPATCH' | translate }}</h5>
              <p class="text-dark-green-muted small px-3">{{ 'LOGIN.POS_DISPATCH_DESC' | translate }}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="col-md-3">
            <div class="step-node">
              <div class="node-circle mx-auto">4</div>
              <h5 class="fw-bold mt-4 text-dark-green">{{ 'LOGIN.PROFIT_REPORT' | translate }}</h5>
              <p class="text-dark-green-muted small px-3">{{ 'LOGIN.PROFIT_REPORT_DESC' | translate }}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="cta-section py-5 text-center text-white">
    <div class="container py-5 z-index-2 position-relative">
      <h2 class="display-5 title-sora fw-bold mb-4">{{ 'LOGIN.CTA_TITLE' | translate }}</h2>
      <p class="lead text-white-50 max-w-600 mx-auto mb-4">{{ 'LOGIN.CTA_DESC' | translate }}</p>

      <div class="d-flex justify-content-center gap-3">
        <button (click)="scrollToOverview()"
          class="btn btn-solid-white rounded-pill px-4 py-3 fw-bold text-uppercase">{{ 'LOGIN.EXPLORE_FEATURES' | translate }}</button>
        <a href="#" class="btn btn-outline-dashed text-white rounded-pill px-4 py-3 fw-bold text-uppercase">{{ 'LOGIN.CONTACT_IT' | translate }}</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <footer class="footer-section py-5 text-white">
    <div class="container px-4">
      <div class="row g-4 align-items-center">
        <div class="col-lg-6 text-center text-lg-start">
          <div class="d-flex align-items-center justify-content-center justify-content-lg-start gap-2 mb-3">
            <div class="logo-circle bg-white p-1">
              <img src="assets/logo.jpg" alt="StockMaster Logo">
            </div>
            <span class="brand-text text-white">StockMaster</span>
          </div>
          <p class="text-white-50 small mb-0 max-w-400">
            {{ 'LOGIN.FOOTER_DESC' | translate }}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div class="col-lg-6">
          <div class="row g-3 text-center text-lg-end">
            <div class="col-4">
              <h6 class="small text-uppercase tracking-wider text-white-50 fw-bold mb-3">{{ 'LOGIN.PLATFORM' | translate }}</h6>
              <ul class="list-unstyled small d-flex flex-column gap-2">
                <li><a href="#" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none hover-white">{{ 'SIDEBAR.DASHBOARD' | translate }}</a></li>
                <li><a href="#" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none hover-white">{{ 'SIDEBAR.INVENTORY' | translate }}</a></li>
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div class="col-4">
              <h6 class="small text-uppercase tracking-wider text-white-50 fw-bold mb-3">COMPANY</h6>
              <ul class="list-unstyled small d-flex flex-column gap-2">
                <li><a href="#" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none hover-white">Terms</a></li>
                <li><a href="#" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none hover-white">{{ 'LOGIN.SECURITY' | translate }}</a></li>
              </ul>
            </div>
            <div class="col-4">
              <h6 class="small text-uppercase tracking-wider text-white-50 fw-bold mb-3">HELP</h6>
              <ul class="list-unstyled small d-flex flex-column gap-2">
                <li><a href="#" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none hover-white">{{ 'LOGIN.CONTACT_SUPPORT' | translate }}</a></li>
                <li><a href="#" class="text-white-50 text-decoration-none hover-white">Guides</a></li>
              </ul>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="text-center text-white-50 small border-top border-white-10 mt-5 pt-4">
        {{ 'LOGIN.COPYRIGHT' | translate }}
      </div>
    </div>
  </footer>

  <!-- Forgot Password Modal -->
  <div class="custom-modal-overlay" *ngIf="showForgotPasswordModal" (click)="closeForgotPassword()">
    <div class="custom-modal" style="max-width:400px; background: white; border-radius: 12px; padding: 24px; color: var(--text);" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h4 class="m-0 fw-bold" style="color: var(--dark-green);">Reset Password</h4>
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm btn-icon" (click)="closeForgotPassword()">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.X" [size]="18"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div *ngIf="resetError" class="alert alert-danger p-2 small mb-3">{{ resetError }}</div>
      <div *ngIf="resetSuccess" class="alert alert-success p-2 small mb-3">{{ resetSuccess }}</div>

      <div *ngIf="!resetSuccess">
        <p class="text-muted small mb-3">Enter your email address and we'll send you a temporary password.</p>
        <div class="form-group mb-4">
          <label class="form-label" style="font-weight: 600; font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 6px; display: block;">Email Address *</label>
          <input type="email" class="form-control" [(ngModel)]="forgotPasswordEmail" placeholder="e.g. user@example.com" style="padding: 10px 14px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--border);" />
        </div>
        <div class="d-flex justify-content-end gap-2">
          <button class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeForgotPassword()">Cancel</button>
          <button class="btn" style="background-color: var(--accent); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 8px; padding: 8px 16px; font-weight: 600;" (click)="submitForgotPassword()" [disabled]="isResetting">
            <span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2" *ngIf="isResetting"></span>
            Send Password
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <div *ngIf="resetSuccess" class="text-center mt-2">
        <button class="btn w-100" style="background-color: var(--accent); color: white; border: none; border-radius: 8px; padding: 10px; font-weight: 600;" (click)="closeForgotPassword()">Got it</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

</div>
```

## File: .\inventory-frontend\src\app\features\login\login.component.ts
```typescript
import { Component } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/services/auth.service';
import { LucideAngularModule, User, Lock, Eye, EyeOff, AlertCircle, TrendingUp, Boxes, Users, X } from 'lucide-angular';
import { TranslateService, TranslatePipe } from '@ngx-translate/core';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-login',
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, LucideAngularModule, TranslatePipe],
  templateUrl: './login.component.html',
  styleUrl: './login.component.css'
})
export class LoginComponent {
  icons = { User, Lock, Eye, EyeOff, AlertCircle, TrendingUp, Boxes, Users, X };
  
  credentials = {
    username: '',
    password: ''
  };
  error: string = '';
  isLoading: boolean = false;
  showPassword = false;

  constructor(
    private authService: AuthService,
    private router: Router,
    private translate: TranslateService
  ) {}

  togglePassword() {
    this.showPassword = !this.showPassword;
  }

  scrollToOverview() {
    document.getElementById('overview')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }

  onSubmit() {
    this.error = '';
    
    if (!this.credentials.username || !this.credentials.password) {
      this.error = this.translate.instant('LOGIN.ERR_FILL_FIELDS');
      return;
    }

    this.isLoading = true;
    this.authService.login(this.credentials).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.router.navigate(['/']);
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isLoading = false;
        this.error = err.error?.message || this.translate.instant('LOGIN.ERR_INVALID_CREDS');
      }
    });
  }

  showForgotPasswordModal = false;
  forgotPasswordEmail = '';
  isResetting = false;
  resetError = '';
  resetSuccess = '';

  openForgotPassword() {
    this.showForgotPasswordModal = true;
    this.forgotPasswordEmail = '';
    this.resetError = '';
    this.resetSuccess = '';
  }

  closeForgotPassword() {
    this.showForgotPasswordModal = false;
  }

  submitForgotPassword() {
    this.resetError = '';
    this.resetSuccess = '';

    if (!this.forgotPasswordEmail) {
      this.resetError = 'Please enter your email.';
      return;
    }

    this.isResetting = true;
    this.authService.forgotPassword(this.forgotPasswordEmail).subscribe({
      next: (res) => {
        this.isResetting = false;
        this.resetSuccess = res.message || 'If that email exists in our system, a temporary password has been sent.';
      },
      error: (err) => {
        this.isResetting = false;
        this.resetError = err.error?.message || 'An error occurred. Please try again.';
      }
    });
  }
}

```


## File: .\inventory-frontend\src\app\features\products\products.component.css
```css
.products-page { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px; }

.filters-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.active-filter {
  background: var(--warning-light) !important;
  color: var(--warning) !important;
  border-color: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3) !important;
}

.sortable {
  cursor: pointer;
  user-select: none;
}

.sortable:hover { color: var(--accent); }

.sku-code {
  background: var(--bg-primary);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 2px 6px;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-family: 'Monaco', monospace;
  color: var(--accent);
}

.product-icon-mini {
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--accent-light);
  color: var(--accent);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.view-toggle {
  display: flex;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  overflow: hidden;
}

.view-btn {
  padding: 7px 10px;
  background: transparent;
  border: none;
  color: var(--text-muted);
  cursor: pointer;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  transition: var(--transition);
}

.view-btn:hover { background: var(--accent-light); color: var(--accent); }
.view-btn.active { background: var(--accent); color: white; }

.spinning { animation: spin 0.7s linear infinite; }

.products-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(240px, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
}

.product-card {
  background: var(--bg-card);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  padding: 20px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 10px;
  transition: var(--transition);
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.product-card:hover {
  border-color: var(--border-hover);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-glow);
  transform: translateY(-2px);
}

.product-card::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(99,102,241,0.04) 0%, transparent 60%);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.product-card-header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.product-card-icon {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--accent-light);
  color: var(--accent);
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
}

.product-card-name {
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--text-primary);
  line-height: 1.3;
  background: none;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: unset;
}

.product-card-sku {
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--accent);
  font-family: monospace;
  background: var(--accent-light);
  padding: 2px 6px;
  border-radius: 4px;
  width: fit-content;
}

.product-card-stats {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.product-card-price {
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: var(--success);
}

.product-card-unit {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--text-muted);
}

.product-card-qty {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 13px;
  color: var(--text-secondary);
  padding: 8px 0;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.product-card-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
}

.product-card-actions .btn {
  flex: 1;
  justify-content: center;
}

@media (max-width: 700px) {
  .page-header {
    align-items: stretch !important;
    gap: 12px !important;
  }

  .page-header > div {
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  .page-header > div:last-child {
    display: grid !important;
    grid-template-columns: auto 1fr;
    gap: 8px !important;
  }

  .page-header > div:last-child .btn {
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
    padding-left: 10px;
    padding-right: 10px;
  }

  .view-toggle {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .view-btn {
    flex: 1;
    justify-content: center;
  }

  .products-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

```


## File: .\inventory-frontend\src\app\features\products\products.component.html
```html
<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px;">
  
  <div class="page-header" style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
     <div>
      <h1 class="page-title">{{ 'PRODUCTS.TITLE' | translate }}</h1>
      <p class="page-subtitle">{{ 'PRODUCTS.ITEMS_COUNT' | translate:{count: totalCount} }}</p>
    </div>
    <div style="display: flex; gap: 12px; align-items: center;">
      
      <div class="view-toggle">
        <button class="view-btn" [ngClass]="{ active: viewMode === 'table' }" (click)="viewMode = 'table'" [title]="'PRODUCTS.LIST_VIEW' | translate">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.List" [size]="16"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
        <button class="view-btn" [ngClass]="{ active: viewMode === 'grid' }" (click)="viewMode = 'grid'" [title]="'PRODUCTS.GRID_VIEW' | translate">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.LayoutGrid" [size]="16"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
      </div>
      <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-secondary" (click)="openImportModal()" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; background-color: var(--bg-card); border: 1px solid var(--border-color); color: var(--text-primary); cursor: pointer;">
        Import Excel
      </button>
      <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-primary" (click)="openAddProduct()" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;">
        <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Plus" [size]="16"></lucide-icon> {{ 'PRODUCTS.ADD_PRODUCT' | translate }}
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card" style="padding: 12px 16px;">
    <div class="search-filter-row">
      
      <div class="search-wrapper" style="min-width: 220px; flex: 1; max-width: 320px;">
        <span class="search-icon"><lucide-icon [img]="icons.Search" [size]="16"></lucide-icon></span>
        <input
          class="search-input"
          [placeholder]="'COMMON.SEARCH' | translate"
          [(ngModel)]="searchQuery"
          (ngModelChange)="onFilterChange()"
        />
      </div>

      <div class="search-filter-controls">
        
        <select class="form-select filter-select" style="width: 160px;" [(ngModel)]="selectedCategory" (ngModelChange)="onFilterChange()">
          <option value="">{{ 'PRODUCTS.CATEGORY' | translate }} ({{ 'COMMON.ALL' | translate }})</option>
          <option *ngFor="let cat of categories" [value]="cat.id">{{ cat.name }}</option>
        </select>

        <select class="form-select filter-select" style="width: 180px;" [(ngModel)]="selectedStockStatus" (ngModelChange)="onFilterChange()">
          <option value="">{{ 'COMMON.STATUS' | translate }} ({{ 'COMMON.ALL' | translate }})</option>
          <option value="in-stock">{{ 'PRODUCTS.IN_STOCK' | translate }}</option>
          <option value="low-stock">{{ 'PRODUCTS.LOW_STOCK' | translate }}</option>
          <option value="out-of-stock">{{ 'PRODUCTS.OUT_OF_STOCK' | translate }}</option>
        </select>

        <button class="btn btn-excel btn-sm" (click)="exportToExcel()" [disabled]="isLoading || getFilteredProducts().length === 0">
          Excel
        </button>
        <button class="btn btn-pdf btn-sm" (click)="exportToPdf()" [disabled]="isLoading || getFilteredProducts().length === 0">
          PDF
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="card" style="padding: 0;" *ngIf="viewMode === 'table'">
    <div class="table-wrapper">
      <table id="products-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>{{ 'COMMON.ID' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'PRODUCTS.PRODUCT_NAME' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'PRODUCTS.SKU' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'PRODUCTS.CATEGORY' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'PRODUCTS.PRICE' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'PRODUCTS.QUANTITY' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'COMMON.STATUS' | translate }}</th>
            <th>{{ 'COMMON.ACTIONS' | translate }}</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          
          <ng-container *ngIf="isLoading">
            <tr *ngFor="let dummy of [1,2,3,4,5]">
              <td *ngFor="let col of [1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]">
                <div class="skeleton" style="height: 16px; width: 70%;"></div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          </ng-container>

          <tr *ngIf="!isLoading && getFilteredProducts().length === 0">
            <td colspan="8">
              <app-empty-state [title]="'PRODUCTS.NO_PRODUCTS' | translate">
                <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Package" [size]="40"></lucide-icon>
              </app-empty-state>
            </td>
          </tr>

          <ng-container *ngIf="!isLoading && getFilteredProducts().length > 0">
            <tr *ngFor="let p of getFilteredProducts()">
              <td [attr.data-label]="'COMMON.ID' | translate" style="font-weight: bold; color: var(--text-muted);">#{{ p.id }}</td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'PRODUCTS.PRODUCT_NAME' | translate">
                                <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;">
                  <div class="product-icon-mini" style="overflow: hidden; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;">
                    <img *ngIf="p.imageUrl" [src]="p.imageUrl" style="width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover;" />
                    <lucide-icon *ngIf="!p.imageUrl" [img]="icons.Package" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 600; font-size: 14px;">{{ p.name }}</div>
                    <div *ngIf="p.supplier" style="font-size: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">{{ p.supplier }}</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'PRODUCTS.SKU' | translate">
                <code class="sku-code">{{ p.sku || '—' }}</code>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'PRODUCTS.CATEGORY' | translate">
                <span class="badge badge-info">{{ p.category }}</span>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'PRODUCTS.PRICE' | translate">
                <span style="font-weight: 600; color: var(--success);">
                  {{ p.price | currency }}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'PRODUCTS.QUANTITY' | translate">
                <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px;">
                  <span style="font-weight: 700;">{{ p.quantity }} <span style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-muted);">{{ p.unit }}</span></span>
                  <span style="font-size: 11px; color: var(--text-muted);">{{ 'PRODUCTS.MIN' | translate }}: {{ p.minQuantity }}</span>
                </div>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'COMMON.STATUS' | translate">
                <span class="badge" [ngClass]="getStockBadgeClass(p.quantity, p.minQuantity)">
                  {{ getStockBadgeText(p.quantity, p.minQuantity) | translate }}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td [attr.data-label]="'COMMON.ACTIONS' | translate">
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 6px;">
                                    <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon btn-sm" (click)="openImageModal(p)" [title]="'PROFILE.UPLOAD_PICTURE' | translate">
                    <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ImagePlus" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
                  </button>
                  <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon btn-sm" (click)="openEditProduct(p)" [title]="'COMMON.EDIT' | translate">
                    <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Edit" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
                  </button>
                  <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-danger btn-icon btn-sm" (click)="confirmDelete(p)" [title]="'COMMON.DELETE' | translate">
                    <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Trash2" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </td>
            </tr>
          </ng-container>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>

    <div class="pagination" *ngIf="totalPages > 0" style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; width: 100%; padding: 16px 24px;">
      <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px;">
        <span class="pagination-info" style="margin: 0;">{{ 'PRODUCTS.PAGE' | translate }} {{ page }} / {{ totalPages }}</span>
        <select class="form-select" style="width: auto; padding: 4px 30px 4px 10px; font-size: 13px;" [(ngModel)]="pageSize" (ngModelChange)="onPageSizeChange()">
          <option [value]="5">5</option>
          <option [value]="10">10</option>
          <option [value]="15">15</option>
          <option [value]="25">25</option>
          <option [value]="50">50</option>
          <option [value]="100">100</option>
        </select>
      </div>
      <div class="pagination-controls">
        <button class="page-btn" [disabled]="page === 1" (click)="prevPage()">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ChevronLeft" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
        <button *ngFor="let pg of getPagesArray()" class="page-btn" [ngClass]="{ active: page === pg }" (click)="goToPage(pg)">
          {{ pg }}
        </button>
        <button class="page-btn" [disabled]="page === totalPages" (click)="nextPage()">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ChevronRight" [size]="14"></lucide-icon>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="products-grid" *ngIf="viewMode === 'grid'">
    
    <ng-container *ngIf="isLoading">
      <div *ngFor="let dummy of [1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8]" class="skeleton" style="height: 180px; border-radius: 12px;"></div>
    </ng-container>

    <ng-container *ngIf="!isLoading">
      <div *ngFor="let p of getFilteredProducts()" class="product-card">
                  <div class="product-card-header">
            <div class="product-card-icon" style="overflow: hidden; padding: 0;">
              <img *ngIf="p.imageUrl" [src]="p.imageUrl" style="width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: cover;" />
              <lucide-icon *ngIf="!p.imageUrl" [img]="icons.Package" [size]="20" style="margin: auto;"></lucide-icon>
            </div>
            <span class="badge" [ngClass]="getStockBadgeClass(p.quantity, p.minQuantity)">
              {{ getStockBadgeText(p.quantity, p.minQuantity) | translate }}
            </span>
          </div>
        <h3 class="product-card-name">{{ p.name }}</h3>
        <p class="product-card-sku">{{ p.sku }}</p>
        <div class="product-card-stats">
          <div>
            <span class="product-card-price">{{ p.price | currency }}</span>
            <span class="product-card-unit">/ {{ p.unit }}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="badge badge-info">{{ p.category }}</span>
        </div>
        <div class="product-card-qty">
          <span>{{ 'PRODUCTS.QUANTITY' | translate }}: <strong>{{ p.quantity }}</strong></span>
          <span class="text-muted">{{ 'PRODUCTS.MIN' | translate }}: {{ p.minQuantity }}</span>
        </div>
                  <div class="product-card-actions">
            <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="openImageModal(p)" [title]="'PROFILE.UPLOAD_PICTURE' | translate">
              <lucide-icon [img]="icons.ImagePlus" [size]="13"></lucide-icon>
            </button>
            <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="openEditProduct(p)">
              <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Edit" [size]="13"></lucide-icon> {{ 'COMMON.EDIT' | translate }}
            </button>
          <button *ngIf="isAdmin" class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" (click)="confirmDelete(p)">
            <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Trash2" [size]="13"></lucide-icon> {{ 'COMMON.DELETE' | translate }}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </ng-container>
  </div>

  <div class="custom-modal-overlay" *ngIf="deleteConfirm" (click)="deleteConfirm = null">
    <div class="custom-modal" style="max-width: 400px;" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="custom-modal-header">
        <h3 class="custom-modal-title" style="color: var(--danger);">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Trash2" [size]="18" style="display: inline; margin-right: 8px;"></lucide-icon>
          {{ 'PRODUCTS.CONFIRM_DELETE_TITLE' | translate }}
        </h3>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-body">
        <p style="color: var(--text-secondary); font-size: 14px;">
          {{ 'PRODUCTS.CONFIRM_DELETE_MSG' | translate }}
        </p>
        <p style="margin-top: 8px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-primary);">
          "{{ deleteConfirm.name }}"
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-footer">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="deleteConfirm = null">{{ 'COMMON.CANCEL' | translate }}</button>
        <button class="btn btn-danger" (click)="deleteProduct()" [disabled]="isDeleting">
          <app-spinner *ngIf="isDeleting" padding="0"></app-spinner>
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Trash2" [size]="14" *ngIf="!isDeleting"></lucide-icon>
          {{ 'COMMON.DELETE' | translate }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="custom-modal-overlay" *ngIf="showProductModal" (click)="showProductModal = false">
    <div class="custom-modal" style="max-width: 500px;" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="custom-modal-header">
        <h3 class="custom-modal-title">
          <lucide-icon [img]="icons.Package" [size]="18" style="display: inline; margin-right: 8px;"></lucide-icon>
          {{ editProduct ? ('PRODUCTS.EDIT_PRODUCT' | translate) : ('PRODUCTS.ADD_PRODUCT' | translate) }}
        </h3>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-body">
        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label">{{ 'PRODUCTS.PRODUCT_NAME' | translate }} *</label>
          <input class="form-input" [(ngModel)]="productForm.name" />
        </div>
        <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px;">
          <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">{{ 'PRODUCTS.SKU' | translate }}</label>
            <input class="form-input" [(ngModel)]="productForm.sku" />
          </div>
          <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">{{ 'PRODUCTS.CATEGORY' | translate }} *</label>
            <select class="form-select" [(ngModel)]="productForm.categoryId">
              <option [ngValue]="null">{{ 'PRODUCTS.SELECT_CATEGORY' | translate }}</option>
              <option *ngFor="let cat of categories" [ngValue]="cat.id">{{ cat.name }}</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr; gap: 12px;">
          <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">{{ 'PRODUCTS.PRICE' | translate }} *</label>
            <input class="form-input" type="number" step="0.01" [(ngModel)]="productForm.price" />
          </div>
          <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">{{ 'PRODUCTS.UNIT' | translate }}</label>
            <input class="form-input" [(ngModel)]="productForm.unit" [placeholder]="'PRODUCTS.UNIT_PLACEHOLDER' | translate" />
          </div>
          <div class="form-group">
            <label class="form-label">{{ 'PRODUCTS.MIN_QTY' | translate }} *</label>
            <input class="form-input" type="number" [(ngModel)]="productForm.minQuantity" />
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="form-group" style="margin-bottom: 0;">
          <label class="form-label">{{ 'COMMON.DESCRIPTION' | translate }}</label>
          <textarea class="form-textarea" rows="2" [(ngModel)]="productForm.description"></textarea>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-footer">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="showProductModal = false">{{ 'COMMON.CANCEL' | translate }}</button>
        <button class="btn btn-primary" (click)="submitProduct()" [disabled]="!productForm.name || !productForm.categoryId || isSavingProduct">
          <app-spinner *ngIf="isSavingProduct" padding="0"></app-spinner>
          {{ 'COMMON.SAVE' | translate }}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <app-profile-picture-modal
    *ngIf="showImageModal"
    [cloudName]="environment.cloudinary.cloudName"
    [uploadPreset]="environment.cloudinary.uploadPreset"
    (close)="closeImageModal()"
    (imageUploaded)="onImageUploaded($event)">
  </app-profile-picture-modal>

  <!-- Import Modal -->
  <div class="custom-modal-overlay" *ngIf="showImportModal" (click)="closeImportModal()">
    <div class="custom-modal" style="max-width: 500px;" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="custom-modal-header">
        <h3 class="custom-modal-title">Bulk Import Products</h3>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-body">
        <p style="font-size: 13px; color: var(--text-secondary); margin-bottom: 16px;">
          Upload an Excel (.xlsx) file to import products. The Excel sheet should contain columns:
          <strong>Name, SKU, Price, PurchasePrice, Quantity, MinQuantity, Category, Brand, Unit, Barcode, Description</strong>.
        </p>
        
        <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
          <a (click)="downloadTemplate()" style="color: var(--primary); cursor: pointer; text-decoration: underline; font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold;">
            Download Template Excel File
          </a>
        </div>

        <div class="form-group">
          <label class="form-label" style="display: block; margin-bottom: 6px; font-weight: 500;">Select Excel File</label>
          <input type="file" (change)="onFileSelected($event)" accept=".xlsx, .xls" class="form-input">
        </div>

        <div *ngIf="importError" class="badge badge-danger" style="margin-top: 12px; font-size: 13px; display: block; padding: 8px; text-align: left; white-space: normal;">
          {{ importError }}
        </div>
        <div *ngIf="importSuccess" class="badge badge-success" style="margin-top: 12px; font-size: 13px; display: block; padding: 8px; text-align: left; white-space: normal;">
          {{ importSuccess }}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="custom-modal-footer">
        <button (click)="closeImportModal()" class="btn btn-ghost" [disabled]="importing">Close</button>
        <button (click)="submitImport()" class="btn btn-primary" [disabled]="importing || !selectedImportFile">
          <app-spinner *ngIf="importing" padding="0"></app-spinner>
          Upload & Import
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```
